+function findPullRequestArtifactsFromArtifacts(
+	artifacts: {
+		id: string;
+		kind: string;
+		externalId: string | null;
+		externalUrl: string | null;
+		createdAt: number;
+	}[],
+): { id: string; number: number; url: string | null }[] {
+	return artifacts
+		.filter(
+			(candidateArtifact) =>
+				candidateArtifact.kind === "pull_request" &&
+				candidateArtifact.externalId,
+		)
+		.map((candidate) => {
+			const number = Number.parseInt(candidate.externalId ?? "", 10);
+			if (!Number.isFinite(number) || number <= 0) {
+				return null;
+			}
 
-	const number = Number.parseInt(candidate.externalId, 10);
-	if (!Number.isFinite(number) || number <= 0) {
-		return null;
+			return {
+				id: candidate.id,
+				number,
+				url: candidate.externalUrl,
+			};
+		})
+		.filter((candidate) => candidate !== null);
+}
+
+async function recordHandoffVerification(
+	client: Client,
+	input: {
+		workItem: GitHubProjectIssueWorkItem;
+		workItemId: string;
+		owner: string;
+		repository: string;
+		existingDecisions?: Decision[];
+	},
+	result: Omit<HandoffVerificationResult, "decisionId" | "workerRunId">,
+): Promise<HandoffVerificationResult> {
+	const freshDecision = findFreshHandoffDecision(
+		input.existingDecisions ?? [],
+		result.outcome,
+		result.selectedArtifact?.pullRequest?.headSha ?? null,
+		result.evidence,
+	);
+	if (freshDecision) {
+		return {
+			...result,
+			decisionId: freshDecision.id,
+			workerRunId: freshDecision.workerRunId,
+		};
 	}
 
+	const workerRun = await createWorkerRun(client, {
+		workItemId: input.workItemId,
+		kind: "post_pr_curator",
+		status: "completed",
+		metadata: {
+			issueNumber: input.workItem.issueNumber,
+			handoffOutcome: result.outcome,
+		},
+	});
+	const decisionId = await createDecision(client, {
+		workItemId: input.workItemId,
+		workerRunId: workerRun.id,
+		phase: "post_pr",
+		outcome: result.outcome,
+		deterministic: true,
+		nextWorkerKind: null,
+		nextAction:
+			result.outcome === "handoff_verified"
+				? "Continue post-PR curation for the verified pull request."
+				: "Stop post-PR automation until the handoff is corrected or can be verified.",
+		evidence: {
+			reason: result.reason,
+			...result.evidence,
+		},
+	});
+
+	await createLink(client, {
+		workItemId: input.workItemId,
+		fromNodeType: "worker_run",
+		fromNodeId: workerRun.id,
+		toNodeType: "decision",
+		toNodeId: decisionId,
+		relation: "evaluates",
+		metadata: {
+			handoffOutcome: result.outcome,
+		},
+	});
+
 	return {
-		id: candidate.id,
-		number,
-		url: candidate.externalUrl,
+		...result,
+		decisionId,
+		workerRunId: workerRun.id,
 	};
 }
 
+function findFreshHandoffDecision(
+	decisions: Decision[],
+	outcome: HandoffVerificationOutcome,
+	headSha: string | null,
+	evidence: Record<string, unknown>,
+): Decision | null {
+	const selected = asRecord(evidence.selectedArtifact);
+	return (
+		decisions.find((decision) => {
+			if (decision.phase !== "post_pr" || decision.outcome !== outcome) {
+				return false;
+			}
+			const decisionEvidence = asRecord(decision.evidence);
+			const observed = asRecord(decisionEvidence?.observed);
+			const decisionSelected = asRecord(decisionEvidence?.selectedArtifact);
+			return (
+				decisionSelected?.number === selected?.number &&
+				(observed?.headSha ?? null) === headSha
+			);
+		}) ?? null
+	);
+}
+
+function getInvalidHandoffReason(input: {
+	owner: string;
+	repository: string;
+	defaultBranch: string;
+	branchPrefix: string;
+	canValidatePolicyFields: boolean;
+	observed: {
+			baseBranch: string;
+			headBranch: string;
+			baseRepositoryOwner: string | null;
+			baseRepositoryName: string | null;
+			hasIssueReference: boolean | null;
+		};
+}): string | null {
+	if (
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryOwner &&
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryOwner !== input.owner
+	) {
+		return "The pull request base repository owner does not match the configured repository.";
+	}
+	if (
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryName &&
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryName !== input.repository
+	) {
+		return "The pull request base repository name does not match the configured repository.";
+	}
+	if (input.observed.baseBranch !== input.defaultBranch) {
+		return "The pull request base branch does not match the configured default branch.";
+	}
+	if (!input.canValidatePolicyFields) {
+		return null;
+	}
+	if (!input.observed.headBranch.startsWith(`${input.branchPrefix}/`)) {
+		return "The pull request head branch does not use the configured Rhapsody branch prefix.";
+	}
+	if (input.observed.hasIssueReference === false) {
+		return "The pull request body or title does not reference the originating issue.";
+	}
+	return null;
+}
+
+function normalizeBranchPrefix(branchPrefix: string): string {
+	return branchPrefix.trim().replace(/\/+$/u, "") || "rhapsody";
+}
+
+function serializeVerificationError(error: unknown) {
+	return error instanceof Error
+		? { name: error.name, message: error.message }
+		: { name: "UnknownError", message: String(error) };
+}
+
 function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
 	return typeof value === "object" && value !== null
 		? (value as Record<string, unknown>)

> AGENT

調べたところ、正しい run は `rhapsody-xi.vercel.app` 側で、builder は sandbox 内で実装と `typecheck` まで進んだものの、Codex timeout で `prSpec: null` / `postflight: null` のまま終わっていました。remote branch / PR handoff は残っていなかったので、ローカルで復元して実装を進めました。

入れた変更:
- post-PR curator に `verifyPullRequestHandoff()` を追加
- `handoff_verified/missing/ambiguous/invalid/unknown` decision を worker graph に記録
- In Progress と Human Review monitoring の入口で handoff verification を必須化
- invalid handoff 時に idempotent な GitHub issue comment を作成
- PR lookup summary に body/repository metadata を追加
- issue reference 判定 helper を追加
- verifier と scheduler のテストを追加/更新

確認:
- `pnpm typecheck` 成功
- `pnpm test lib/scheduler/tick.test.ts lib/workers/post-pr-curator.test.ts -- --run` 成功
- `pnpm vitest lib/workers/integration-repair/wrapper.test.ts --run --testTimeout=15000` 成功
- `pnpm test -- --run` は変更範囲外の `integration-repair/wrapper.test.ts` がデフォルト 5 秒 timeout で失敗します。同じテストは 15 秒 timeout では通っています。

   103	export async function verifyPullRequestHandoff(
   104		client: Client,
   105		input: {
   106			workItem: GitHubProjectIssueWorkItem;
   107			workItemId: string;
   108			owner: string;
   109			repository: string;
   110			defaultBranch: string;
   111			branchPrefix: string;
   112			artifacts: Artifact[];
   113			existingDecisions?: Decision[];
   114			getPullRequest?: typeof getPullRequest;
   115		},
   116	): Promise<HandoffVerificationResult> {
   117		const candidates = findPullRequestArtifactsFromArtifacts(input.artifacts);
   118		const expectedBranchPrefix = normalizeBranchPrefix(input.branchPrefix);
   119		const baseEvidence = {
   120			issueNumber: input.workItem.issueNumber,
   121			artifactCount: candidates.length,
   122			artifactIds: candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id),
   123			expected: {
   124				owner: input.owner,
   125				repository: input.repository,
   126				baseBranch: input.defaultBranch,
   127				branchPrefix: expectedBranchPrefix,
   128			},
   129		};
   130	
   131		if (candidates.length === 0) {
   132			return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
   133				outcome: "handoff_missing",
   134				reason:
   135					"No usable pull request artifact was recorded for this work item.",
   136				selectedArtifact: null,
   137				evidence: baseEvidence,
   138			});
   139		}
   140	
   141		if (candidates.length > 1) {
   142			return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
   143				outcome: "handoff_ambiguous",
   144				reason:
   145					"Multiple pull request artifacts were recorded, so Rhapsody could not safely choose one.",
   146				selectedArtifact: null,
   147				evidence: baseEvidence,
   148			});
   149		}
   150	
   151		const selectedArtifact = candidates[0];
   152		let pullRequest: PullRequestSummary;
   153		try {
   154			pullRequest = await (input.getPullRequest ?? getPullRequest)({
   155				owner: input.owner,
   156				repository: input.repository,
   157				pullRequestNumber: selectedArtifact.number,
   158			});
   159		} catch (error) {
   160			return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
   161				outcome: "handoff_unknown",
   162				reason: "GitHub pull request lookup failed while verifying the handoff.",
   163				selectedArtifact: { ...selectedArtifact, pullRequest: null },
   164				evidence: {
   165					...baseEvidence,
   166					selectedArtifact,
   167					error: serializeVerificationError(error),
   168				},
   169			});
   170		}
   171	
   172		const observed = {
   173			pullRequestNumber: pullRequest.number,
   174			pullRequestUrl: pullRequest.htmlUrl,
   175			baseBranch: pullRequest.baseRef,
   176			headBranch: pullRequest.headRef,
   177			headSha: pullRequest.headSha ?? null,
   178			baseRepositoryOwner: pullRequest.baseRepositoryOwner ?? null,
   179			baseRepositoryName: pullRequest.baseRepositoryName ?? null,
   180			headRepositoryOwner: pullRequest.headRepositoryOwner ?? null,
   181			headRepositoryName: pullRequest.headRepositoryName ?? null,
   182			hasIssueReference:
   183				pullRequest.body === undefined
   184					? null
   185					: containsIssueReference(
   186							`${pullRequest.title}\n\n${pullRequest.body ?? ""}`,
   187							input.workItem.issueNumber,
   188							{ owner: input.owner, name: input.repository },
   189						),
   190		};
   191		const invalidReason = getInvalidHandoffReason({
   192			owner: input.owner,
   193			repository: input.repository,
   194			defaultBranch: input.defaultBranch,
   195			branchPrefix: expectedBranchPrefix,
   196			canValidatePolicyFields: pullRequest.body !== undefined,
   197			observed,
   198		});
   199		const selected = { ...selectedArtifact, pullRequest };
   200	
   201		if (invalidReason) {
   202			return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
   203				outcome: "handoff_invalid",
   204				reason: invalidReason,
   205				selectedArtifact: selected,
   206				evidence: {
   207					...baseEvidence,
   208					selectedArtifact,
   209					observed,
   210				},
   211			});
   212		}
   213	
   214		return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
   215			outcome: "handoff_verified",
   216			reason:
   217				"The pull request handoff matches the current work item and repository boundaries.",
   218			selectedArtifact: selected,
   219			evidence: {
   220				...baseEvidence,
   221				selectedArtifact,
   222				observed,
   223			},
   224		});
   225	}
   226	
   227	export async function runPostPrCurator(
   228		client: Client,
   229		input: {
   230			workItem: GitHubProjectIssueWorkItem;
  1080			toNodeType: "decision",
  1081			toNodeId: decisionId,
  1082			relation: "evaluates",
  1083			metadata: {
  1084				handoffOutcome: result.outcome,
  1085			},
  1086		});
  1087	
  1088		return {
  1089			...result,
  1090			decisionId,
  1091			workerRunId: workerRun.id,
  1092		};
  1093	}
  1094	
  1095	function findFreshHandoffDecision(
  1096		decisions: Decision[],
  1097		outcome: HandoffVerificationOutcome,
  1098		headSha: string | null,
  1099		evidence: Record<string, unknown>,
  1100	): Decision | null {
  1101		const selected = asRecord(evidence.selectedArtifact);
  1102		return (
  1103			decisions.find((decision) => {
  1104				if (decision.phase !== "post_pr" || decision.outcome !== outcome) {
  1105					return false;
  1106				}
  1107				const decisionEvidence = asRecord(decision.evidence);
  1108				const observed = asRecord(decisionEvidence?.observed);
  1109				const decisionSelected = asRecord(decisionEvidence?.selectedArtifact);
  1110				return (
  1111					decisionSelected?.number === selected?.number &&
  1112					(observed?.headSha ?? null) === headSha
  1113				);
  1114			}) ?? null
  1115		);
  1116	}
  1117	
  1118	function getInvalidHandoffReason(input: {
  1119		owner: string;
  1120		repository: string;
  1121		defaultBranch: string;
  1122		branchPrefix: string;
  1123		canValidatePolicyFields: boolean;
  1124		observed: {
  1125			baseBranch: string;
  1126			headBranch: string;
  1127			baseRepositoryOwner: string | null;
  1128			baseRepositoryName: string | null;
  1129			hasIssueReference: boolean | null;
  1130		};
  1131	}): string | null {
  1132		if (
  1133			input.observed.baseRepositoryOwner &&
  1134			input.observed.baseRepositoryOwner !== input.owner
  1135		) {
  1136			return "The pull request base repository owner does not match the configured repository.";
  1137		}
  1138		if (
  1139			input.observed.baseRepositoryName &&
  1140			input.observed.baseRepositoryName !== input.repository
  1141		) {
  1142			return "The pull request base repository name does not match the configured repository.";
  1143		}
  1144		if (input.observed.baseBranch !== input.defaultBranch) {
  1145			return "The pull request base branch does not match the configured default branch.";
  1146		}
  1147		if (!input.canValidatePolicyFields) {
  1148			return null;
  1149		}
  1150		if (!input.observed.headBranch.startsWith(`${input.branchPrefix}/`)) {
  1151			return "The pull request head branch does not use the configured Rhapsody branch prefix.";
  1152		}
  1153		if (input.observed.hasIssueReference === false) {
  1154			return "The pull request body or title does not reference the originating issue.";
  1155		}
  1156		return null;
  1157	}
  1158	
  1159	function normalizeBranchPrefix(branchPrefix: string): string {
  1160		return branchPrefix.trim().replace(/\/+$/u, "") || "rhapsody";
  1161	}
  1162	
  1163	function serializeVerificationError(error: unknown) {
  1164		return error instanceof Error
  1165			? { name: error.name, message: error.message }

   824	async function commentOnInvalidHandoff(input: {
   825		client: Client;
   826		config: ReturnType<typeof loadRhapsodyConfig>;
   827		dependencies: SchedulerPostPrDependencies;
   828		item: GitHubProjectIssueWorkItem;
   829		handoffVerification: HandoffVerificationResult;
   830	}) {
   831		if (input.handoffVerification.outcome !== "handoff_invalid") {
   832			return;
   833		}
   834	
   835		const selected = input.handoffVerification.selectedArtifact;
   836		const pullRequest = selected?.pullRequest;
   837		const marker = `<!-- rhapsody:handoff-invalid:${selected?.number ?? "unknown"}:${pullRequest?.headSha ?? "unknown"} -->`;
   838		const body = [
   839			marker,
   840			"Rhapsody could not accept the pull request handoff for this item.",
   841			"",
   842			`Reason: ${input.handoffVerification.reason}`,
   843			"",
   844			"Observed:",
   845			`- Pull request: ${selected ? `#${selected.number}` : "unknown"}`,
   846			`- Base branch: ${pullRequest?.baseRef ?? "unknown"}`,
   847			`- Head branch: ${pullRequest?.headRef ?? "unknown"}`,
   848			`- Expected base branch: ${input.config.repository.defaultBranch}`,
   849			`- Expected branch prefix: ${input.config.repository.branchPrefix}`,
   850			"",
   851			"Rhapsody will not run post-PR checks, repair, auto-merge, or move this item forward automatically until this handoff is corrected or reviewed by a human.",
   852		].join("\n");
   853	
   854		try {
   855			const comments = await (
   856				input.dependencies.fetchIssueComments ?? fetchIssueComments
   857			)({
   858				owner: input.config.repository.owner,
   859				repository: input.config.repository.name,
   860				issueNumber: input.item.issueNumber,
   861			});
   862			if (comments.some((comment) => comment.body.includes(marker))) {
   863				return;
   864			}
   865			await (input.dependencies.createIssueComment ?? createIssueComment)({
   866				owner: input.config.repository.owner,
   867				repository: input.config.repository.name,
   868				issueNumber: input.item.issueNumber,
   869				body,
   870			});
   871		} catch (error) {
   872			await createEvent(input.client, {
   873				runId: null,
   874				attemptId: null,
   875				level: "warn",
   876				type: "scheduler.invalid_handoff_comment_failed",
   877				message: "Scheduler could not create an invalid handoff issue comment.",
   878				data: {
   879					issueNumber: input.item.issueNumber,
   880					pullRequestNumber: selected?.number ?? null,
   881					error: serializeError(error),
   882				},
   883			});
   884		}
   885	}

Two concrete issues stood out.\n\n1. **High — `handoff_missing`/`handoff_ambiguous` are not idempotent across ticks**\n   - In fresh-decision reuse, the matcher requires a `selectedArtifact.number` plus `observed.headSha`:\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/workers/post-pr-curator/index.ts:1091`\n   - For `handoff_missing` and `handoff_ambiguous`, there is no `selectedArtifact`, so this check never matches and a new `worker_run` + `decision` is created every scheduler pass.\n   - In the scheduler flow, both in-progress and human-review handlers always call verification on each pass, so these outcomes keep re-recording:\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/scheduler/tick.ts:625`\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/scheduler/tick.ts:645`\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/scheduler/tick.ts:745`\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/scheduler/tick.ts:766`\n   - This contradicts the idempotency expectation for durable scheduler state.\n\n2. **Medium — non-missing invalid outcomes are treated as “handled” and not surfaced as skipped**\n   - `runPostPrCuratorForInProgress` and `runPostPrCuratorForHumanReview` only mark `missing_pr_artifact` as skipped; they return handled for `handoff_ambiguous|handoff_invalid|handoff_unknown`:\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/scheduler/tick.ts:649`\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/scheduler/tick.ts:772`\n   - Net effect: those items won’t appear in `skippedIssues` even though no automation progress can occur. That makes scheduler-level observability noisier/less actionable and can hide repeated handoff blocks.\n\n3. **Test gap — repeated-pass/idempotency behavior is untested for added handoff outcomes**\n   - Current `post-pr-curator` tests validate each outcome once, not re-validation behavior over multiple ticks:\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/workers/post-pr-curator.test.ts:417`\n   - Existing scheduler coverage only checks one pass for missing artifact:\n     - `/Users/toyamarinyon/Documents/rhapsody/lib/scheduler/tick.test.ts:404`\n   - Missing regression tests for:\n     - repeated scheduler passes for `handoff_missing/ambiguous/unknown` (should not accumulate decisions),\n     - and `commentOnInvalidHandoff` idempotent comment emission under repeated runs.\n\nI also ran `npm test` on the touched suites and `npm run typecheck`; both pass, so these are behavioral/idempotency issues rather than compile/test failures."}},"timed_out":false}

+	handoffVerification: HandoffVerificationResult;
+}) {
+	if (input.handoffVerification.outcome !== "handoff_invalid") {
+		return;
+	}
+
+	const selected = input.handoffVerification.selectedArtifact;
+	const pullRequest = selected?.pullRequest;
+	const marker = `<!-- rhapsody:handoff-invalid:${selected?.number ?? "unknown"}:${pullRequest?.headSha ?? "unknown"} -->`;
+	const body = [
+		marker,
+		"Rhapsody could not accept the pull request handoff for this item.",
+		"",
+		`Reason: ${input.handoffVerification.reason}`,
+		"",
+		"Observed:",
+		`- Pull request: ${selected ? `#${selected.number}` : "unknown"}`,
+		`- Base branch: ${pullRequest?.baseRef ?? "unknown"}`,
+		`- Head branch: ${pullRequest?.headRef ?? "unknown"}`,
+		`- Expected base branch: ${input.config.repository.defaultBranch}`,
+		`- Expected branch prefix: ${input.config.repository.branchPrefix}`,
+		"",
+		"Rhapsody will not run post-PR checks, repair, auto-merge, or move this item forward automatically until this handoff is corrected or reviewed by a human.",
+	].join("\n");
+
+	try {
+		const comments = await (
+			input.dependencies.fetchIssueComments ?? fetchIssueComments
+		)({
+			owner: input.config.repository.owner,
+			repository: input.config.repository.name,
+			issueNumber: input.item.issueNumber,
+		});
+		if (comments.some((comment) => comment.body.includes(marker))) {
+			return;
+		}
+		await (input.dependencies.createIssueComment ?? createIssueComment)({
+			owner: input.config.repository.owner,
+			repository: input.config.repository.name,
+			issueNumber: input.item.issueNumber,
+			body,
+		});
+	} catch (error) {
+		await createEvent(input.client, {
+			runId: null,
+			attemptId: null,
+			level: "warn",
+			type: "scheduler.invalid_handoff_comment_failed",
+			message: "Scheduler could not create an invalid handoff issue comment.",
+			data: {
+				issueNumber: input.item.issueNumber,
+				pullRequestNumber: selected?.number ?? null,
+				error: serializeError(error),
+			},
+		});
+	}
+}

 	config: ReturnType<typeof loadRhapsodyConfig>;

+function buildPullRequestArtifact(id: string, externalId: string) {
+	return {
+		id,
+		workItemId: "unused",
+		workerRunId: "wrn_builder",
+		kind: "pull_request",
+		externalId,
+		externalUrl: `https://github.com/toyamarinyon/rhapsody/pull/${externalId}`,
+		snapshot: {},
+		metadata: {},
+		createdAt: 1,
+		updatedAt: 1,
+	};
+}
+
+function buildPullRequest(
+	number: number,
+	overrides: Partial<PullRequestSummary> = {},
+): PullRequestSummary {
+	return {
+		reused: true,
+		number,
+		htmlUrl: `https://github.com/toyamarinyon/rhapsody/pull/${number}`,
+		title: `Fixes #${number === 404 ? 130 : 127}`,
+		body: `Resolves #${number === 404 ? 130 : 127}`,
+		headRef: `rhapsody/issue-${number === 404 ? 130 : 127}-1`,
+		headSha: `head-${number}`,
+		baseRef: "main",
+		baseSha: "base",
+		headRepositoryOwner: "toyamarinyon",
+		headRepositoryName: "rhapsody",
+		baseRepositoryOwner: "toyamarinyon",
+		baseRepositoryName: "rhapsody",
+		state: "open" as const,
+		merged: false,
+		mergedAt: null,
+		...overrides,
+	};
+}

+export async function verifyPullRequestHandoff(
+	client: Client,
+	input: {
+		workItem: GitHubProjectIssueWorkItem;
+		workItemId: string;
+		owner: string;
+		repository: string;
+		defaultBranch: string;
+		branchPrefix: string;
+		artifacts: Artifact[];
+		existingDecisions?: Decision[];
+		getPullRequest?: typeof getPullRequest;
+	},
+): Promise<HandoffVerificationResult> {
+	const candidates = findPullRequestArtifactsFromArtifacts(input.artifacts);
+	const expectedBranchPrefix = normalizeBranchPrefix(input.branchPrefix);
+	const baseEvidence = {
+		issueNumber: input.workItem.issueNumber,
+		artifactCount: candidates.length,
+		artifactIds: candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id),
+		expected: {
+			owner: input.owner,
+			repository: input.repository,
+			baseBranch: input.defaultBranch,
+			branchPrefix: expectedBranchPrefix,
+		},
+	};
+
+	if (candidates.length === 0) {
+		return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
+			outcome: "handoff_missing",
+			reason:
+				"No usable pull request artifact was recorded for this work item.",
+			selectedArtifact: null,
+			evidence: baseEvidence,
+		});
+	}
+
+	if (candidates.length > 1) {
+		return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
+			outcome: "handoff_ambiguous",
+			reason:
+				"Multiple pull request artifacts were recorded, so Rhapsody could not safely choose one.",
+			selectedArtifact: null,
+			evidence: baseEvidence,
+		});
+	}
+
+	const selectedArtifact = candidates[0];
+	let pullRequest: PullRequestSummary;
+	try {
+		pullRequest = await (input.getPullRequest ?? getPullRequest)({
+			owner: input.owner,
+			repository: input.repository,
+			pullRequestNumber: selectedArtifact.number,
+		});
+	} catch (error) {
+		return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
+			outcome: "handoff_unknown",
+			reason: "GitHub pull request lookup failed while verifying the handoff.",
+			selectedArtifact: { ...selectedArtifact, pullRequest: null },
+			evidence: {
+				...baseEvidence,
+				selectedArtifact,
+				error: serializeVerificationError(error),
+			},
+		});
+	}
+
+	const observed = {
+		pullRequestNumber: pullRequest.number,
+		pullRequestUrl: pullRequest.htmlUrl,
+		baseBranch: pullRequest.baseRef,
+		headBranch: pullRequest.headRef,
+		headSha: pullRequest.headSha ?? null,
+		baseRepositoryOwner: pullRequest.baseRepositoryOwner ?? null,
+		baseRepositoryName: pullRequest.baseRepositoryName ?? null,
+		headRepositoryOwner: pullRequest.headRepositoryOwner ?? null,
+		headRepositoryName: pullRequest.headRepositoryName ?? null,
+		hasIssueReference:
+			pullRequest.body === undefined
+				? null
+				: containsIssueReference(
+						`${pullRequest.title}\n\n${pullRequest.body ?? ""}`,
+						input.workItem.issueNumber,
+						{ owner: input.owner, name: input.repository },
+					),
+	};
+	const invalidReason = getInvalidHandoffReason({
+		owner: input.owner,
+		repository: input.repository,
+		defaultBranch: input.defaultBranch,
+		branchPrefix: expectedBranchPrefix,
+		canValidatePolicyFields: pullRequest.body !== undefined,
+		observed,
+	});
+	const selected = { ...selectedArtifact, pullRequest };
+
+	if (invalidReason) {
+		return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
+			outcome: "handoff_invalid",
+			reason: invalidReason,
+			selectedArtifact: selected,
+			evidence: {
+				...baseEvidence,
+				selectedArtifact,
+				observed,
+			},
+		});
+	}
+
+	return recordHandoffVerification(client, input, {
+		outcome: "handoff_verified",
+		reason:
+			"The pull request handoff matches the current work item and repository boundaries.",
+		selectedArtifact: selected,
+		evidence: {
+			...baseEvidence,
+			selectedArtifact,
+			observed,
+		},
+	});
+}

 export async function runPostPrCurator(
 	client: Client,
 	input: {
@@ -839,27 +989,189 @@ export function findPullRequestArtifactFromArtifacts(

 ): { id: string; number: number; url: string | null } | null {

+}

+function findPullRequestArtifactsFromArtifacts(
+	artifacts: {
+		id: string;
+		kind: string;
+		externalId: string | null;
+		externalUrl: string | null;
+		createdAt: number;
+	}[],
+): { id: string; number: number; url: string | null }[] {
+	return artifacts
+		.filter(
+			(candidateArtifact) =>
+				candidateArtifact.kind === "pull_request" &&
+				candidateArtifact.externalId,
+		)
+		.map((candidate) => {
+			const number = Number.parseInt(candidate.externalId ?? "", 10);
+			if (!Number.isFinite(number) || number <= 0) {
+				return null;
+			}
 
-	const number = Number.parseInt(candidate.externalId, 10);
-	if (!Number.isFinite(number) || number <= 0) {
-		return null;
+			return {
+				id: candidate.id,
+				number,
+				url: candidate.externalUrl,
+			};
+		})
+		.filter((candidate) => candidate !== null);
+}
+
+async function recordHandoffVerification(
+	client: Client,
+	input: {
+		workItem: GitHubProjectIssueWorkItem;
+		workItemId: string;
+		owner: string;
+		repository: string;
+		existingDecisions?: Decision[];
+	},
+	result: Omit<HandoffVerificationResult, "decisionId" | "workerRunId">,
+): Promise<HandoffVerificationResult> {
+	const freshDecision = findFreshHandoffDecision(
+		input.existingDecisions ?? [],
+		result.outcome,
+		result.selectedArtifact?.pullRequest?.headSha ?? null,
+		result.evidence,
+	);
+	if (freshDecision) {
+		return {
+			...result,
+			decisionId: freshDecision.id,
+			workerRunId: freshDecision.workerRunId,
+		};
 	}
 
+	const workerRun = await createWorkerRun(client, {
+		workItemId: input.workItemId,
+		kind: "post_pr_curator",
+		status: "completed",
+		metadata: {
+			issueNumber: input.workItem.issueNumber,
+			handoffOutcome: result.outcome,
+		},
+	});
+	const decisionId = await createDecision(client, {
+		workItemId: input.workItemId,
+		workerRunId: workerRun.id,
+		phase: "post_pr",
+		outcome: result.outcome,
+		deterministic: true,
+		nextWorkerKind: null,
+		nextAction:
+			result.outcome === "handoff_verified"
+				? "Continue post-PR curation for the verified pull request."
+				: "Stop post-PR automation until the handoff is corrected or can be verified.",
+		evidence: {
+			reason: result.reason,
+			...result.evidence,
+		},
+	});
+
+	await createLink(client, {
+		workItemId: input.workItemId,
+		fromNodeType: "worker_run",
+		fromNodeId: workerRun.id,
+		toNodeType: "decision",
+		toNodeId: decisionId,
+		relation: "evaluates",
+		metadata: {
+			handoffOutcome: result.outcome,
+		},
+	});
+
 	return {
-		id: candidate.id,
-		number,
-		url: candidate.externalUrl,
+		...result,
+		decisionId,
+		workerRunId: workerRun.id,
 	};
 }
 
+function findFreshHandoffDecision(
+	decisions: Decision[],
+	outcome: HandoffVerificationOutcome,
+	headSha: string | null,
+	evidence: Record<string, unknown>,
+): Decision | null {
+	const selected = asRecord(evidence.selectedArtifact);
+	return (
+		decisions.find((decision) => {
+			if (decision.phase !== "post_pr" || decision.outcome !== outcome) {
+				return false;
+			}
+			const decisionEvidence = asRecord(decision.evidence);
+			const observed = asRecord(decisionEvidence?.observed);
+			const decisionSelected = asRecord(decisionEvidence?.selectedArtifact);
+			if (outcome === "handoff_missing" || outcome === "handoff_ambiguous") {
+				return true;
+			}
+			if (outcome === "handoff_unknown") {
+				return true;
+			}
+			return (
+				decisionSelected?.number === selected?.number &&
+				(observed?.headSha ?? null) === headSha
+			);
+		}) ?? null
+	);
+}
+
+function getInvalidHandoffReason(input: {
+	owner: string;
+	repository: string;
+	defaultBranch: string;
+	branchPrefix: string;
+	canValidatePolicyFields: boolean;
+	observed: {
+		baseBranch: string;
+		headBranch: string;
+		baseRepositoryOwner: string | null;
+		baseRepositoryName: string | null;
+		hasIssueReference: boolean | null;
+	};
+}): string | null {
+	if (
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryOwner &&
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryOwner !== input.owner
+	) {
+		return "The pull request base repository owner does not match the configured repository.";
+	}
+	if (
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryName &&
+		input.observed.baseRepositoryName !== input.repository
+	) {
+		return "The pull request base repository name does not match the configured repository.";
+	}
+	if (input.observed.baseBranch !== input.defaultBranch) {
+		return "The pull request base branch does not match the configured default branch.";
+	}
+	if (!input.canValidatePolicyFields) {
+		return null;
+	}
+	if (!input.observed.headBranch.startsWith(`${input.branchPrefix}/`)) {
+		return "The pull request head branch does not use the configured Rhapsody branch prefix.";
+	}
+	if (input.observed.hasIssueReference === false) {
+		return "The pull request body or title does not reference the originating issue.";
+	}
+	return null;
+}
+
+function normalizeBranchPrefix(branchPrefix: string): string {
+	return branchPrefix.trim().replace(/\/+$/u, "") || "rhapsody";
+}
+
+function serializeVerificationError(error: unknown) {
+	return error instanceof Error
+		? { name: error.name, message: error.message }
+		: { name: "UnknownError", message: String(error) };
+}
+
 function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
 	return typeof value === "object" && value !== null
 		? (value as Record<string, unknown>)

export type SchedulerTickDependencies = {
	config?: ReturnType<typeof loadRhapsodyConfig>;
	fetchProjectIssueWorkItems?: typeof fetchProjectIssueWorkItems;

};

const ACTIVE_STATUSES = ["Todo", "In Progress"];
const RUNNING_PROJECT_STATUS = "In Progress";

export async function runSchedulerTick(
	client: Client,
	dependencies: SchedulerTickDependencies = {},

		});

	}
}

> AGENT

レビューと修正まで完了しました。

`Volta` の指摘:
- `handoff_missing/ambiguous/unknown` の decision reuse が甘く、tick ごとに重複記録されうる
- invalid/ambiguous/unknown が scheduler の `skippedIssues` に出ない
- repeated-pass と invalid comment idempotency のテスト不足

`builder` に修正依頼後、こちらでさらに確認して、残っていた invalid comment テストの穴も直しました。今は invalid handoff comment が 1 回だけ作成され、再 tick では重複しないことをテストしています。

確認済み:
- `pnpm typecheck` 成功
- `pnpm test lib/workers/post-pr-curator.test.ts lib/scheduler/tick.test.ts -- --run` 成功
- `pnpm vitest lib/workers/integration-repair/wrapper.test.ts --run --testTimeout=15000` 成功
- `pnpm test -- --run` は既知の `lib/workers/integration-repair/wrapper.test.ts` がデフォルト 5 秒 timeout で失敗しますが、同テストは 15 秒 timeout で通っています。
